## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T10:41:49.301Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now I understand the ItemSlot right-click gate. Key conditions for bag opening:
- `!PlayerInput.UsingGamepadUI && context == 0 && ItemID.Sets.OpenableBag[inv[slot].type]` → context 0 = inventory
- `Main.mouseItem.IsAir || !Item.CanStack(inv[slot], Main.mouseItem)` — mouse item is air or can't stack with slot item
- `Main.mouseRight` held and `Main.mouseRightRelease` (edge trigger)
- Then TryOpenContainer: rolls → grant items → stack-- → if stack==0 SetDefaults(0) → PlaySound(7) → stackSplit=30 → mouseRightRelease=false

// 原版物品 id → 注册 key/内部 id 的统一查表入口(本地物品退役后全库唯一物品字面量源)。
// ★ 严禁手拼 vi_ 键字符串:蛇形/驼峰双注册下唯一权威是 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID(驼峰)。
// 后续 dual-key 清理(蛇形/驼峰归一)只需改本文件,消费点无感。
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

/** 高频消费点语义常量(grep 唯一入口;数值 = 原版 item id 权威) */
export const VI_KEY = {
  // 基础方块/材料
  DIRT: VI(2), STONE: VI(3), WOOD: VI(9), SAND: VI(169), SNOW: VI(593),
  CLAY: VI(133), GLASS: VI(170), MUD: VI(176), OBSIDIAN: VI(173), SILT: VI(424),
  ICE: VI(664), GRAY_BRICK: VI(129), RED_BRICK: VI(131),
  GEL: VI(23), ACORN: VI(27), MUSHROOM: VI(5), LENS: VI(38), ROPE: VI(965),
  BOMB: VI(166), WIRE: VI(530),
  // 放置/功能物
  TORCH: VI(8), PLATFORM: VI(94), DOOR: VI(25), WORKBENCH: VI(36), FURNACE: VI(33),
  ANVIL: VI(35), CHEST: VI(48), SIGN: VI(171),
  TABLE: VI(32), CHAIR: VI(34), BED: VI(224), TOMBSTONE: VI(321), LIFE_CRYSTAL: VI(29),
  // 桶族/水蜡
  EMPTY_BUCKET: VI(205), WATER_BUCKET: VI(206), LAVA_BUCKET: VI(207), HONEY_BUCKET: VI(1128),
  WATER_CANDLE: VI(148),
  // 药水(常用)
  LESSER_HEAL: VI(28), HEAL: VI(188),

  SUSPICIOUS_EYE: VI(43), WORM_FOOD: VI(70), BLOODY_SPINE: VI(1331), SLIME_CROWN: VI(560),

1	// 宝藏袋开包引擎（Player.OpenBossBag :6828-7520,1.4.5.6 全 19 袋）
2	// 数据 = tools/extract-bossbags.mjs 结构化提取（提取期归一条件,随机全部留给引擎期掷）。
3	// 引擎执行:逐 spawn 求值 conds(chance/master/noExtra/noItem/switch 选支),
4	// 支持 idFrom 范围/dedupPair/maskIds/pick2/stackExpr 表达式 +
5	// 共享尾段（按 boss value 附赠钱币,贪心拆面额）。
6	import bagsJson from './vanilla-bossbags.json';
7	import { npcValueOf } from '../drops/NpcDrops';
8	
9	interface Cond { t: string; n?: number; id?: number; k?: number; branch?: number; elseOf?: number; pair?: number }
10	interface Spawn {
11	  id?: number; stack?: number; lo?: number; hi?: number;
12	  idFrom?: [number, number]; dedup2?: boolean;
13	  maskIds?: number[]; maskRoll?: number;
14	  pick2?: number[]; stackExpr?: Array<[number, number]>;
15	  stackVar?: boolean; crim?: boolean; evil?: number; good?: number;
16	  conds: Cond[];
17	}
18	const DATA = bagsJson as {
19	  bags: Record<string, { devArmor: boolean; spawns: Spawn[] }>;
20	  coinMap: Record<string, number>;
21	};
22	
23	export const BOSS_BAG_IDS = new Set(Object.keys(DATA.bags).map(Number));
24	
25	/** rand.Next(a,b) 上界开 */
26	const rn = (a: number, b: number) => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a));
27	
28	export function openBossBag(
29	  vid: number,
30	  give: (id: number, stack?: number) => void,
31	  world: { masterMode: boolean; crimson: boolean; extraAccessory: boolean; hasItem: (id: number) => boolean },
32	): void {
33	  const bag = DATA.bags[String(vid)];
34	  if (!bag) return;
35	  // bag.devArmor: TryGettingDevArmor 开发者套装小概率——未实装登记
36	  // switch 掷一次（同袋内所有 sw cond 共享各自 switch 的结果）
37	  const swPick = new Map<number, number>();
38	  // else 补集:同一 chanceId 的 else 与 if 共享掷骰结果
39	  const chanceRoll = new Map<number, boolean>();
40	  let chanceSeq = 0;
41	  for (const sp of bag.spawns) {
42	    let ok = true;
43	    for (const c of sp.conds) {
44	      switch (c.t) {
45	        case 'chance': {
46	          const r = Math.random() < 1 / (c.n ?? 2);
47	          chanceRoll.set(c.pair ?? ++chanceSeq, r);
48	          ok &&= r;
49	          break;
50	        }
51	        case 'chanceElse': {
52	          const pair = c.pair ?? ++chanceSeq;
53	          if (!chanceRoll.has(pair)) chanceRoll.set(pair, Math.random() < 0.5);
54	          ok &&= !chanceRoll.get(pair)!;
55	          break;
56	        }
57	        case 'master': ok &&= world.masterMode; break;
58	        case 'noExtra': ok &&= !world.extraAccessory; break;
59	        case 'noItem': ok &&= !world.hasItem(c.id ?? 0); break;
60	        case 'sw': {
61	          const k = c.k ?? 0;
62	          if (!swPick.has(k)) swPick.set(k, Math.floor(Math.random() * 8));
63	          ok &&= (c.branch ?? -1) === swPick.get(k);
64	          break;
65	        }
66	        default: break;
67	      }
68	      if (!ok) break;
69	    }
70	    if (!ok) continue;
71	    // ---- 表达式掷骰与产出 ----
72	    if (sp.pick2) {
73	      const pool = [...sp.pick2];
74	      for (let k = 0; k < 2 && pool.length; k++) {
75	        const ix = Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length);
76	        give(pool[ix]);
77	        pool.splice(ix, 1);
78	      }
79	      continue;
80	    }
81	    if (sp.maskIds) {

84	      continue;
85	    }

88	      if (sp.stackVar) return sp.stack;
89	      if (sp.lo !== undefined && sp.hi !== undefined) return rn(sp.lo, sp.hi);
90	      return sp.stack ?? 1;
91	    };
92	    if (sp.crim) {
93	      give(world.crimson ? (sp.evil ?? 0) : (sp.good ?? 0), sp.stackVar ? stackOf() : 1);
94	      continue;
95	    }
96	    if (sp.idFrom) {
97	      const a = rn(sp.idFrom[0], sp.idFrom[1]);
98	      give(a);
99	      if (sp.dedup2) {
100	        let b = rn(sp.idFrom[0], sp.idFrom[1]);
101	        let g = 0;
102	        while (b === a && g++ < 20) b = rn(sp.idFrom[0], sp.idFrom[1]);
103	        give(b);
104	      }
105	      continue;
106	    }
107	    if (sp.id === undefined) continue;
108	    give(sp.id, stackOf());

117	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * chance) === 0) value *= 1 + rn(lo, hi) * 0.01;
118	  }
119	  let remain = Math.floor(value);
120	  for (const [coin, unit] of [[74, 1000000], [73, 10000], [72, 100], [71, 1]] as const) {
121	    const n = Math.floor(remain / unit);
122	    if (n > 0) { give(coin, n); remain -= n * unit; }
123	  }
124	}

772	  /** 一键装备（ItemSlot.SwapEquip/ArmorSwap :3022-3346 近似）：部位→armor[0-2]（时装→[10-12]），
773	   *  配饰→armor[3-9] 第一个空槽/原槽交换；染料→dye 第一个空槽；杂项→对应 miscEquips 槽；
774	   *  成功后取消收藏（原版 :3341） */
775	  private swapEquipItem(cur: { id: number; stack: number }, where: SlotWhere, i: number): void {
776	    const inv = this.game.player.inv;
777	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[cur.id];
778	    const src = where === 'inv' ? inv.slots[i] : where === 'armor' ? inv.armor[i] : inv.dye[i];
779	    if (!src) return;
780	    let target = -1;
781	    let targetWhere: SlotWhere = 'armor';
782	    if (d.armor) {
783	      target = d.armor.slot;
784	    } else if (armorSlotIndexOfInternal(cur.id) >= 0) {
785	      target = armorSlotIndexOfInternal(cur.id); // vi_ 盔甲（vanilla-itemstats 部位）
786	    } else if (d.accessory || statOfInternal(cur.id)?.acc !== undefined) {
787	      // vi_ 配饰走 itemstats acc 判定(legacy accessory 字段已随退役消失——曾只认
788	      // legacy 字段导致 vi_ 配饰右键一键装备静默失效)
789	      for (let k = 3; k < inv.accSlotMax; k++) {
790	        if (!inv.armor[k]) { target = k; break; }
791	      }
792	      if (target < 0) target = 3; // 全满→交换第 3 槽
793	    } else {
794	      const kind = equipKindOfInternal(cur.id);
795	      if (kind === 'dye') {
796	        // 染料→dye[0-9] 第一个空槽（SwapEquip :3030 dye 分支）
797	        for (let k = 0; k < inv.dye.length; k++) {
798	          if (!inv.dye[k]) { target = k; break; }
799	        }
800	        targetWhere = 'dye';
801	        if (target < 0) return;
802	      } else if (kind && MISC_KINDS.includes(kind)) {
803	        target = MISC_KINDS.indexOf(kind);
804	        targetWhere = 'misc';
805	      } else return;
806	    }
807	    if (targetWhere === 'armor' && !inv.armorAccepts(target, cur.id)) return;
808	    src.favorited = false; // 装上取消收藏（原版 ArmorSwap :3341）
809	    const arr = targetWhere === 'armor' ? inv.armor : targetWhere === 'dye' ? inv.dye : inv.miscEquips;
810	    const tmp = arr[target];
811	    arr[target] = src;
812	    if (where === 'inv') inv.slots[i] = tmp ?? null;
813	    else if (where === 'armor') inv.armor[i] = tmp ?? null;
814	    else if (where === 'dye') inv.dye[i] = tmp ?? null;
815	    else if (where === 'misc') inv.miscEquips[i] = tmp ?? null;
816	    else inv.miscDyes[i] = tmp ?? null;
817	    this.game.sfx.play('menuTick', 1);
818	    // 成就：装备态检查（AchievementsHelper.HandleOnEquip）
819	    this.game.achNotifyEquipChanged();
820	  }
821	
822	  /** Shift+左键快速转移（GetAlternateClickAction :563-662 主干）：
823	   *  开箱→入箱（仅堆已有或空格）；装备/配饰/染料/杂项→卸回背包；箱内→背包；背包内→垃圾桶 */
824	  private shiftClick(i: number, where: SlotWhere): void {
825	    const inv = this.game.player.inv;
826	    if (where === 'armor' || where === 'dye' || where === 'misc' || where === 'miscDye') {
827	      // Unequip（:634-649）：卸回背包
828	      const arr = this.equipArray(where);
829	      const cur = arr[i];
830	      if (!cur || cur.favorited) return;
831	      const left = inv.add(cur.id, cur.stack);
832	      if (left === 0) arr[i] = null;
833	      this.refreshAll();
834	      return;
835	    }
836	    if (where === 'inv') {
837	      const cur = inv.slots[i];
838	      if (!cur || cur.favorited) return; // 收藏禁转移（:566）
839	      if (this.openChest) {
840	        // TryPlacingInChest（QuickStack 语义：先合并箱内已有类型再空格）
841	        const chest = this.openChest;
842	        let moved = 0;
843	        for (let k = 0; k < chest.items.length; k++) {
844	          const cs = chest.items[k];
845	          if (cs && cs.id === cur.id && cs.stack < ITEM_DEFS[cur.id].maxStack) {
846	            const put = Math.min(ITEM_DEFS[cur.id].maxStack - cs.stack, cur.stack);
847	            cs.stack += put; cur.stack -= put; moved += put;
848	            if (cur.stack <= 0) break;
849	          }
850	        }
851	        for (let k = 0; k < chest.items.length && cur.stack > 0; k++) {
852	          if (!chest.items[k]) { chest.items[k] = cur; inv.slots[i] = null; return; }
853	        }
854	        if (cur.stack <= 0) inv.slots[i] = null;
855	        if (moved > 0 || !inv.slots[i]) this.refreshAll();
856	        return;
857	      }
858	      // 无箱→垃圾桶（覆盖旧内容，OverrideLeftClick cursorOverride 6 :689-706）
859	      inv.trash = cur;
860	      inv.slots[i] = null;
861	      this.game.sfx.play('menuTick', 1);
862	      this.refreshAll();
863	      return;
864	    }
865	    if (where === 'chest') {
866	      // TransferFromChest（:626-633）：箱→背包
867	      const chest = this.openChest;
868	      if (!chest) return;
869	      const cur = chest.items[i];
870	      if (!cur) return;
871	      const left = inv.add(cur.id, cur.stack);
872	      if (left === 0) chest.items[i] = null;
873	      else cur.stack = left;
874	      this.refreshAll();
875	    }
876	  }
877	
878	  // ---- 拖拽系统：按下起点 + 光标持有物（ghost 跟随） ----
879	  private dragFrom: { i: number; where: SlotWhere; sx: number; sy: number } | null = null;

26	/** Main.hslToRgb（h∈[0,1)） */
27	function hslToRgb(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
28	  const c = (1 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1)) * s;
29	  const hp = ((h % 1) + 1) % 1 * 6;
30	  const x = c * (1 - Math.abs((hp % 2) - 1));
31	  let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
32	  if (hp < 1) { r = c; g = x; } else if (hp < 2) { r = x; g = c; }
33	  else if (hp < 3) { g = c; b = x; } else if (hp < 4) { g = x; b = c; }
34	  else if (hp < 5) { r = x; b = c; } else { r = c; b = x; }
35	  const m = l - c / 2;
36	  return [Math.round((r + m) * 255), Math.round((g + m) * 255), Math.round((b + m) * 255)];
37	}

46	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;

48	  return st.isSolid(x, y) || st.half[i] !== 0 || st.slope[i] !== 0;

53	  return solidOrSloped(st, x, y);

65	  if (blockPortals(st, x + yOff, y - xOff)
66	    || blockPortals(st, x + yOff - xOff, y - xOff - yOff)
67	    || blockPortals(st, x + yOff + xOff, y - xOff + yOff)) return false;

72	  return canPlacePortalOn(st, x, y) && canPlacePortalOn(st, x - xOff, y - yOff) && canPlacePortalOn(st, x + xOff, y + yOff)
73	    && st.slope[i] === st.slope[i2] && st.slope[i] === st.slope[i3];
74	}

78	  if (isValidLine(st, x, y, xOff, yOff)) return [x, y];
79	  if (isValidLine(st, x - xOff, y - yOff, xOff, yOff)) return [x - xOff, y - yOff];
80	  if (isValidLine(st, x + xOff, y + yOff, xOff, yOff)) return [x + xOff, y + yOff];

86	function findCollisionTile(st: TileStore, sx: number, sy: number, ex: number, ey: number): [number, number] | null {
87	  const dist = Math.hypot(ex - sx, ey - sy);
88	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dist / 8));
89	  for (let s = 1; s <= steps; s++) {

92	    const ty = Math.floor((sy + (ey - sy) * t) / 16);
93	    if (solidOrSloped(st, tx, ty)) return [tx, ty];
94	  }

118	  }
119	
120	  get centerX(): number { return this.x + 5; }
121	  get centerY(): number { return this.y + 5; }
122	
123	  /** 门线两端（GetPortalEdges：中心 ± 22×angle 向量） */
124	  edges(): [number, number, number, number] {
125	    const dx = Math.cos(this.angle) * 22, dy = Math.sin(this.angle) * 22;
126	    return [this.centerX - dx, this.centerY - dy, this.centerX + dx, this.centerY + dy];
127	  }
128	
129	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
130	    if (this.cooldown > 0) this.cooldown--;
131	    this.animT++;
132	    if (this.animT >= 4) { this.animT = 0; this.anim = (this.anim + 1) % 10; } // 原版 frameCounter 4tick 步进
133	    // 穿越判定（TryGoingThroughPortals :105-215）
134	    const p = game.player;
135	    if (p.dead || this.cooldown > 0) return;
136	    const [x1, y1, x2, y2] = this.edges();
137	    const pb = { x: p.x + p.vx, y: p.y + p.vy, w: p.w, h: p.h };
138	    if (!segHitsRect(x1, y1, x2, y2, pb.x - 2, pb.y - 2, pb.w + 4, pb.h + 4)) return;
139	    const other = portalRegistry().find((o) => o !== this && !o.dead && o.owner === this.owner && o.form !== this.form);
140	    if (!other) return;
141	    const st = game.world.store;
142	    // 出口点（GetPortalOutingPoint :372-394，返回实体左上落点）+ 沿出口法线推开原距离（:133-136）
143	    const [ox, oy, bx, by] = portalOutingPoint(p.w, p.h, other.centerX, other.centerY, other.angle);
144	    const dist = Math.hypot(p.cx - this.centerX, p.cy - this.centerY);
145	    const bl = Math.hypot(bx, by) || 1;
146	    const tx = ox + (bx / bl) * dist, ty = oy + (by / bl) * dist;
147	    // 四向 16px 扫掠净空（:138-161：任一方向受阻则放弃本次穿越）
148	    for (const [dx, dy] of [[16, 0], [-16, 0], [0, 16], [0, -16]] as const) {
149	      if (!sweepFree(st, tx, ty, dx, dy, p.w, p.h)) return;
150	    }
151	    // 速度改向（:163-186）：保速率，指向出口法线；零速给 0.1 最小推力
152	    let speed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
153	    if (speed < 0.1) speed = 0.1;
154	    let dirX = bx / bl, dirY = by / bl;
155	    if (bx === 0 && by === 0) { dirX = p.facing || 1; dirY = 0; }
156	    p.vx = dirX * speed;
157	    p.vy = dirY * speed;
158	    if (by === -1 && p.vy > -0.1) p.vy = -0.1;  // 顶部门最小上抛（:177-180）
159	    p.x = tx;
160	    p.y = ty;
161	    this.cooldown = 10;
162	    other.cooldown = 10;
163	  }
164	
165	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
166	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
167	    if (!ctx) return;
168	    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_602.png');
169	    const [cr, cg, cb] = portalColor(this.form);
170	    ctx.save();
171	    ctx.translate(this.centerX, this.centerY);
172	    ctx.rotate(this.angle);
173	    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
174	      const frame = Portal.tintedFrame(img, this.anim, cr, cg, cb);
175	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.95;
176	      ctx.drawImage(frame, -9, -25);
177	    } else {
178	      // 贴图未就位回退：门色椭圆环（原版门基色 A=66/255，:407）
179	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
180	      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgb(${cr},${cg},${cb})`;
181	      ctx.lineWidth = 4;
182	      ctx.beginPath();
183	      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 6, 22, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
184	      ctx.stroke();
185	    }
186	    ctx.restore();
187	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
188	  }
189	
190	  /** 帧着色缓存（multiply + destination-in 保形；10 帧 × 2 色） */
191	  private static tintedFrame(tex: HTMLImageElement, frame: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
192	    const key = `${tex.src}|${frame}|${r},${g},${b}`;
193	    const hit = Portal.frameCache.get(key);
194	    if (hit) return hit;
195	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
196	    c.width = 18; c.height = 20;
197	    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
198	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 20, 18, 20, 0, 0, 18, 20);
199	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
200	    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
201	    cx.fillRect(0, 0, 18, 20);
202	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
203	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 20, 18, 20, 0, 0, 18, 20);
204	    Portal.frameCache.set(key, c);
205	    return c;
206	  }
207	}
208	
209	/** 门注册表（props 桶同一实例；AddPortal 的 RemoveMyOldPortal/相交清理在此做） */
210	const registry: Portal[] = [];
211	export function portalRegistry(): Portal[] { return registry; }
212	export function clearPortals(): void { for (const p of registry) p.dead = true; registry.length = 0; }
213	
214	/** 线段 × 矩形（Liang-Barsky 裁剪；Collision.CheckAABBvLineCollision 近似） */
215	function segHitsRect(x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number, rx: number, ry: number, rw: number, rh: number): boolean {
216	  let t0 = 0, t1 = 1;
217	  const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
218	  const clip = (p: number, q: number): boolean => {
219	    if (p === 0) return q >= 0;
220	    const r = q / p;
221	    if (p < 0) { if (r > t1) return false; if (r > t0) t0 = r; }
222	    else { if (r < t0) return false; if (r < t1) t1 = r; }
223	    return true;
224	  };
225	  return clip(-dx, x1 - rx) && clip(dx, rx + rw - x1) && clip(-dy, y1 - ry) && clip(dy, ry + rh - y1);
226	}
227	
228	/** 矩形区域无实心块 */
229	function boxFree(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
230	  const x0 = Math.floor(x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((x + w - 0.01) / TILE);
231	  const y0 = Math.floor(y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((y + h - 0.01) / TILE);
232	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
233	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
234	      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;
235	    }
236	  }
237	  return true;
238	}
239	
240	/** 四向净空扫掠（TryGoingThroughPortals :138-161：从出口-16px 向出口 16px 步进的
241	 *  TileCollision 探测——整段无碰撞才放行；8px 步进采样近似） */
242	function sweepFree(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, dx: number, dy: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
243	  for (let s = -16; s <= 16; s += 8) {
244	    if (!boxFree(st, x + dx * s, y + dy * s, w, h)) return false;
245	  }
246	  return true;
247	}
248	
249	/** GetPortalOutingPoint（:372-394 1:1）：按门朝向 8 分桶定出口偏移与法线 (bx,by) */
250	export function portalOutingPoint(w: number, h: number, px: number, py: number, angle: number): [number, number, number, number] {
251	  // MathHelper.WrapAngle 归一到 (-PI, PI]（atan2(1,0)+PI/2 = PI 会折到 -PI → default 桶）
252	  const a = ((angle + Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI) - Math.PI;
253	  const num = Math.round(a / (Math.PI / 4));
254	  switch (num) {
255	    case -2: case 2:
256	      return [px + (num === 2 ? -w : 0), py - h / 2, num === 2 ? -1 : 1, 0];
257	    case 0: case 4:
258	      return [px - w / 2, py + (num === 0 ? 0 : -h), 0, num === 0 ? 1 : -1];
259	    case -3: case 3:
260	      return [px + (num === -3 ? 0 : -w), py - h, num === -3 ? 1 : -1, -1];
261	    case -1: case 1:
262	      return [px + (num === -1 ? 0 : -w), py, num === -1 ? 1 : -1, 1];
263	    default:
264	      return [px, py, 0, 0];
265	  }
266	}
267	
268	/** TryPlacingPortal（:218-248 1:1）+ AddPortal（:328-340）：解算落点并落门。
269	 *  boltX/boltY = 弹体左上 px；vx/vy = 撞击时速度；form = 门色 0 橙 / 1 蓝。
270	 *  返回新门（已入注册表；同 form 旧门与相交门标记 dead），失败返回 null */
271	export function placePortal(st: TileStore, boltX: number, boltY: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number): Portal | null {
272	  const vlen = Math.hypot(vx, vy);
273	  if (vlen < 0.001) return null;
274	  const dx = vx / vlen, dy = vy / vlen;
275	  // FindCollision：弹位 → 弹位+速度+方向*32（:222）
276	  const hit = findCollisionTile(st, boltX + 5, boltY + 5, boltX + 5 + vx + dx * 32, boltY + 5 + vy + dy * 32);
277	  if (!hit) return null;
278	  const [tx, ty] = hit;
279	  const ti = st.idx(tx, ty);
280	  const slope = st.slope[ti];
281	  const half = st.half[ti] !== 0;
282	  for (let i = 0; i < (half ? 2 : EDGES.length); i++) {
283	    const [ex, ey] = EDGES[i]!;
284	    if (ex * dx + ey * dy <= 0) continue;   // Vector2.Dot(EDGES[i], vector) > 0
285	    const best = findValidLine(st, tx, ty, ey, -ex);
286	    if (!best) continue;
287	    // 位置 = 命中行中心 - 法线×8（半砖不偏移）；角度 = atan2(edge)+PI/2（:241-243）
288	    const cx = best[0] * 16 + 8 - ex * (half ? 0 : 8);
289	    const cy = best[1] * 16 + 8 - ey * (half ? 0 : 8);
290	    return addPortal(cx, cy, Math.atan2(ey, ex) + Math.PI / 2, form, dx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
291	  }
292	  if (slope !== 0) {
293	    const [sx2, sy2] = SLOPE_EDGES[slope - 1]!;
294	    if (sx2 * -dx + sy2 * -dy > 0) {
295	      const [ox2, oy2] = SLOPE_OFFSETS[slope - 1]!;
296	      const best = findValidLine(st, tx, ty, -oy2, ox2);
297	      if (best) {
298	        return addPortal(best[0] * 16 + 8, best[1] * 16 + 8, Math.atan2(sy2, sx2) - Math.PI / 2, form, dx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
299	      }
300	    }
301	  }
302	  return null;
303	}
304	
305	/** AddPortal（:328-353）：同 form 旧门移除 + 门线相交门移除（:355-378） */
306	function addPortal(x: number, y: number, angle: number, form: number, direction: number): Portal {
307	  for (let i = registry.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
308	    const o = registry[i]!;
309	    if (o.dead) { registry.splice(i, 1); continue; }
310	    if (o.form === form) o.dead = true;                        // RemoveMyOldPortal
311	    else if (portalLinesIntersect(o, x, y, angle)) o.dead = true; // RemoveIntersectingPortals
312	  }
313	  for (let i = registry.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (registry[i]!.dead) registry.splice(i, 1);
314	  const p = new Portal(x, y, angle, form);
315	  p.vx = direction; // direction 留档（原版 projectile.direction）
316	  registry.push(p);
317	  return p;
318	}
319	
320	/** Collision.CheckLinevLine（门线相交；:360-377） */
321	function portalLinesIntersect(a: Portal, bx: number, by: number, bAngle: number): boolean {
322	  const [ax1, ay1, ax2, ay2] = a.edges();
323	  const dx = Math.cos(bAngle) * 22, dy = Math.sin(bAngle) * 22;
324	  const [bx1, by1, bx2, by2] = [bx - dx, by - dy, bx + dx, by + dy];
325	  const d1x = ax2 - ax1, d1y = ay2 - ay1, d2x = bx2 - bx1, d2y = by2 - by1;
326	  const den = d1x * d2y - d1y * d2x;
327	  if (den === 0) return false;
328	  const t = ((bx1 - ax1) * d2y - (by1 - ay1) * d2x) / den;
329	  const u = ((bx1 - ax1) * d1y - (by1 - ay1) * d1x) / den;
330	  return t >= 0 && t <= 1 && u >= 0 && u <= 1;
331	}
332

````
For item 3384: itemCombat has `{autoReuse, knockBack:2, noMelee:true, shoot:600, shootSpeed:24, useAnimation:20, useTime:20}` — no damage field! Note: Item.cs case 3384 doesn't set damage (only knockBack 2f). So damage = 0 → base.damage = c.damage ?? 1 = 1. And projectile 600 aiStyle = 75. Is 75 in the AI_* classification constants? No: the classifier checks AI_GLOWSTICK/AI_GRENADE/AI_BOOMERANG/AI_SPEAR/AI_SHORTSWORD/AI_ANCHORED/AI_YOYO/AI_FLAIL. aiStyle 75 doesn't match, no melee/magic, noMelee=true → falls to `if (c.melee || c.ranged || c.noMelee) return { kind: 'shot', ... }` → kind 'shot' with shoot 600, damage 1.

So currently item 3384 IS classified as 'shot' and the generic shot case spawns an `Arrow(px, py, cos*24, sin*24, 1, 2, 600, ...)` — an invisible-ish arrow flying at 24px/tick with no portal logic. That's the gap.

- My territory: src/entities/Portal.ts, src/data/, new files.

---draw fn---
  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
    if (st.invisible) return; // 733 alpha=255：隐形纯发射器（SetDefaults :7440）
    // 烟花族按 form 选色变体贴图；单贴图 kind 走 proj。
    // ★ensureVImage 触发懒加载(VANILLA_MISC 瘦身后弹幕表不再预载,直取会永久色块)
    const tex = st.projs?.[this.form] ?? st.proj;
    const img = tex && r.atlas ? r.atlas.ensureVImage(tex) : null;
    if (img && st.fw && st.fh && !TrapShot.isBlank(r, tex!)) {
      // 原版弹幕贴图(整帧):飞行类贴图默认朝上(镖尖/火苗向上),对齐速度方向需 +90°;
      // 尖刺球/巨石不随速度旋转,按位移滚转
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
      if (st.spin) ctx.rotate(this.rot);
      else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (st.rotOff ?? 0));
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, st.fw, st.fh, -st.fw / 2, -st.fh / 2, st.fw, st.fh);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }

        drawn = true;
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (drawn) return;
    // 贴图未就绪:短线兜底(方向与弹速一致)
    ctx.strokeStyle = st.fallback ?? '#C8B89A';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.lineTo(this.cx - this.vx * 1.6, this.cy - this.vy * 1.6);
    ctx.stroke();

next(n: number): number;\n}\nconst defaultRand: BagRand = { next: (n) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n) };\n\n/** QuickSpawnItem 出口：give(原版 item id, stack)。调用方负责入包/落地 */\nexport type BagGive = (id: number, stack?: number) => void;\n\n/** OpenPresent（Player.cs:6651-6770）：圣诞礼盒 1869 十九支 else-if 链。\n *  ★链式判定：每支独立掷 Next(n)，前支命中即 return——顺序与概率逐字，\n *  不得改写为独立 if（否则后支概率被前支吞掉）。 */\nfunction openPresent(give: BagGive, hardMode: boolean, rnd: BagRand): void {\n  if (rnd.next(15) === 0 && hardMode) {                       // :6654 雪球仪 602（硬模式门）\n    give(602);\n  } else if (rnd.next(30) === 0) {                            // :6658 煤块 1922\n    give(1922);\n  } else if (rnd.next(400) === 0) {                           // :6662 狗哨 1927\n    give(1927);\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6666-6669 红莱德步枪 1870 + 火枪弹 97×30-60\n    give(1870);\n    give(97, rnd.next(30, 61));\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6671 糖杖剑 1909\n    give(1909);\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6675 糖杖镐 1917\n    give(1917);\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6679 糖杖钩 1915\n    give(1915);\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6683 水果蛋糕回力镖 1918\n    give(1918);\n  } else if (rnd.next(150) === 0) {                           // :6687 暖手宝 1921\n    give(1921);\n  } else if (rnd.next(300) === 0) {                           // :6691 工具箱 1923\n    give(1923);\n  } else if (rnd.next(40) === 0) {                            // :6695 驯鹿角 1907\n    give(1907);\n  } else if (rnd.next(10) === 0) {                            // :6699 冬青 1908\n    give(1908);\n  } else if (rnd.next(15) === 0) {                            // :6703-6728 圣诞套装 switch(Next(5))\n    switch (rnd.next(5)) {\n      case 0: give(1932); give(1933); give(1934); break;      // 圣诞夫人三件\n      case 1: give(1935); give(1936); give(1937); break;      // 派克大衣三件\n      case 2: give(1940); give(1941); give(1942); break;      // 树装三件\n      case 3: give(1938); break;                              // 雪帽\n      default: give(1939); break;                             // 丑毛衣\n    }\n  } else if (rnd.next(7) === 0) {                             // :6730-6745 食物 switch(Next(3))\n    switch (rnd.next(3)) {\n      case 0: give(1911); break;                              // 圣诞布丁\n      case 1: give(1919); break;                              // 糖霜饼干\n      default: give(1920); break;                             // 姜饼\n    }\n  } else if (rnd.next(8) === 0) {                             // :6747-6749 蛋奶酒 1912×1-3\n    give(1912, rnd.next(1, 4));\n  } else if (rnd.next(9) === 0) {                             // :6751-6753 八角 1913×20-40\n    give(1913, rnd.next(20, 41));\n  } else {                                                    // :6755-6769 兜底建材 switch(Next(3))\n    switch (rnd.next(3)) {\n      case 0: give(1872, rnd.next(20, 50)); break;            // 松木块\n      case 1: give(586, rnd.next(20, 50)); break;             // 糖杖块\n      default: give(591, rnd.next(20, 50)); break;            // 绿糖杖块\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** OpenLegacyPresent（Player.cs:6772-6788）：旧版三色礼盒 599/600/601——单掷 14 支 */\nfunction openLegacyPresent(give: BagGive, hardMode: boolean, rnd: BagRand): void {\n  const num = rnd.next(14);                                   // :6775\n  if (num === 0 && hardMode) {                                // :6776-6779 0/14 雪球仪（硬模式门）\n    give(602);\n  } else if (num <= 7) {                                      // :6780-6783 1-7 → 糖杖块\n    give(586, rnd.next(20, 50));\n  } else {                                                    // :6784-6787 8-13 → 绿糖杖块\n    give(591, rnd.next(20, 50));\n  }\n}\n\n/** OpenGoodieBag（Player.cs:9199-9282）：礼袋 1774。前四支独立掷后 early return\n *  （:9205/9210/9215/9220），兜底 switch(Next(19)) 十九套装（QuickSpawnItem_CostumeSet\n *  :9175-9186 = 逐件 QuickSpawnItem） */\nfunction openGoodieBag(give: BagGive, rnd: BagRand): void {\n  if (rnd.next(150) === 0) { give(1810); return; }            // :9202-9205 不幸毛线 1810\n  if (rnd.next(150) === 0) { give(1800); return; }            // :9207-9210 蝙蝠钩 1800\n  if (rnd.next(4) === 0) { give(1809, rnd.next(10, 41)); return; } // :9212-9215 臭蛋 1809×10-40\n  if (rnd.next(10) === 0) { give(rnd.next(1846, 1851)); return; }  // :9217-9220 挂画 1846-1850\n  switch (rnd.next(19)) {                                     // :9222-9281 化装套装\n    case 0: give(1749); give(1750); give(1751); break;        // 猫套装\n    case 1: give(1746); give(1747); give(1748); break;        // 海盗套装\n    case 2: give(1752); give(1753); break;                    // 忍者兜帽/衣\n    case 3: give(1767); give(1768); give(1769); break;        // 新娘三件\n    case 4: give(1770); give(1771); break;                    // 南瓜人头/衣\n    case 5: give(1772); give(1773); break;                    // 女巫扫帚/魔杖（鬼魂腿）\n    case 6: give(1754); give(1755); give(1756); break;        // 科学怪人三件\n    case 7: give(1757); give(1758); give(1759); break;        // 狼人三件\n    case 8: give(1760); give(1761); give(1762); break;        // 吸血鬼三件\n    case 9: give(1763); give(1764); give(1765); break;        // 僵尸西装三件\n    case 10: give(1766); give(1775); give(1776); break;       // 女巫帽+裙+靴\n    case 11: give(1777); give(1778); break;                   // 稻草人头/衣\n    case 12: give(1779); give(1780); give(1781); break;       // 棒棒少女三件\n    case 13: give(1819); give(1820); break;                   // 收割者兜帽/长袍\n    case 14: give(1821); give(1822); give(1823); break;       // 猫女郎三件\n    case 15: give(1824); break;                               // 猫耳\n    case 16: give(1838); give(1839); give(1840); break;       // 太空生物三件\n    case 17: give(1841); give(1842); give(1843); break;       // 狼人（蓝毛）三件\n    default: give(1851); give(1852); break;                   // 寻宝者衣/裤\n  }\n}\n\n/** OpenHerbBag（Player.cs:9064-9138）：草药袋 3093——2-4 件（1/3 +1），每件\n *  Next(14) 七草七籽映射 + 2-4 栈（1/3 再 +1-4） */\nfunction openHerbBag(give: BagGive, rnd: BagRand): void {\n  let num = rnd.next(2, 5);                                   // :9067 件数 2-4\n  if (rnd.next(3) === 0) num++;                               // :9068-9071 1/3 → +1\n  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {                             // :9072\n    let id = rnd.next(14);                                    // :9074\n    switch (id) {                                             // :9075-9130 十四支映射\n      case 0: id = 313; break;   // 日耀花\n      case 1: id = 314; break;   // 月光草\n      case 2: id = 315; break;   // 蔓生根\n      case 3: id = 317; break;   // 睡莲叶\n      case 4: id = 316; break;   // 死草\n      case 5: id = 318; break;   // 火焰花\n      case 6: id = 2358; break;  // 颤悚荆\n      case 7: id = 307; break;   // 日耀花籽\n      case 8: id = 308; break;   // 月光草籽\n      case 9: id = 309; break;   // 蔓生根籽\n      case 10: id = 311; break;  // 睡莲叶籽\n      case 11: id = 310; break;  // 死草籽\n      case 12: id = 312; break;  // 火焰花籽\n      default: id = 2357; break; // 颤悚荆籽\n    }\n    let stack = rnd.next(2, 5);                               // :9131 栈 2-4\n    if (rnd.next(3) === 0) stack += rnd.next(1, 5);           // :9132-9134 1/3 → +1-4\n    give(id, stack);\n  }\n}\n\n/** 右键开包分派（TryOpenContainer_GrantItems :1662-1721 同源子集）：\n *  返回 true = 已开（调用方做 stack-- + PlaySound(7)）；false = 非本引擎物品\n *  （宝藏袋/木匣/钓鱼箱等走各自链，UI 应回落原右键行为） */\nexport function openBagItem(\n  vid: number,\n  give: BagGive,\n  world: { hardMode: boolean },\n  rng: BagRand = defaultRand,\n): boolean {\n  switch (vid) {\n    case 1869: openPresent(give, world.hardMode, rng); return true;\n    case 599: case 600: case 601: openLegacyPresent(give, world.hardMode, rng); return true;\n    case 1774: openGoodieBag(give, rng); return true;\n    case 3093: openHerbBag(give, rng); return true;\n    default: return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** give 出口的 id 是否在本仓已注册（防 vanilla.json 缺条目静默吞掉） */\nexport function bagItemIdRegistered(id: number): boolean {\n  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id] !== undefined;\n}\n"
}

/** vi_<id> key → 原版 item id 反解 */
export function vanillaIdOfItemKey(key: string): number {
  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

```

36	  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {

```ts
fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
  this.life--;
  if (this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
  const color = portalColorHex[this.form]; // for particles
  const [lr, lg, lb] = portalColor(this.form);
  let updates = PortalGunBolt.EXTRA_UPDATES;   // :15331 numUpdates = extraUpdates
  while (updates >= 0 && !this.dead) {          // :15332-15334
    updates--;
    // ---- AI（:51174-51228）----
    game.addLightAt?.(this.cx + this.vx * 3, this.cy + this.vy * 3, lr*0.5/255, lg*0.5/255, lb*0.5/255); // :51176-51179
    if (this.alpha > 0 && this.alpha <= 15) {   // :51180-51203 4 尘 + 音（ai[0] 分支）
      game.spawnBurst?.(...4, speed 6, dir, 0.35, {life 14, damp 0.94});
      game.playSfx(this.form === 0 ? 'portalOrange' : 'portalBlue', 1, this.x, this.y); // :51195-51202
    }
    this.alpha -= 15; if (this.alpha < 0) this.alpha = 0;  // :51204-51208
    if (++this.frameCounter >= 4) { this.frameCounter = 0; this.frame = (this.frame + 1) % 2; } // :51209-51214 (projFrames[601]=2)
    if (this.alpha === 0) {  // :51217-51227 尘 263 拖尾（原版每子步 1 粒；粒子池 600 上限 → 每 2 子步 1 粒，同 TrapShot :299 惯例）
      ...every 2nd substep spawnBurst 1 particle speed 0.75
    }
    // ---- 移动 + 碰撞（Update 主体序：AI 后 position += velocity）----
    const lvx = this.vx, lvy = this.vy;         // lastVelocity
    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
    if (!game.world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; break; }  // 出界消亡

      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;                                 // :16689
      this.dead = true;                                                     // :16690 Kill()
      break;
    }
  }
}

}

while(numUpdates>=0)）：\n// 每 tick \"AI+移动+碰撞\" 整轮跑 31 次 → 弹速 3px×31 = 93px/tick（近瞬时光束，撞块检测\n// 逐子步进行不穿墙）。\nimport { Entity } from './Entity';\nimport type { GameHooks } from './types';\nimport type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';\nimport type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';\nimport { TILE } from '../core/constants';\nimport { portalColor } from './Portal';\n\n/** 601 SetDefaults（:6225-6226）碰撞盒 */\nconst BOLT_W = 10;\nconst BOLT_H = 10;\n/** :6230 extraUpdates = 30（每 tick 子步数 = extraUpdates+1 = 31） */\nconst EXTRA_UPDATES = 30;\n/** :63904 num49 = 3f（600 发 601 的单子步速度） */\nconst BOLT_SPEED = 3;\n/** SetDefaults 不写 timeLeft → 默认 3600 */\nconst BOLT_LIFE = 3600;\n\n/** 601 PortalGunBolt（物品 3384 / 传送门炮共用弹型；物品链由此类承载） */\nexport class PortalGunBolt extends Entity {\n  w = BOLT_W;\n  h = BOLT_H;\n  /** ai[0] = 门色 form（0 橙 / 1 蓝）。物品链：左键 0 / 右键 1（Player.cs:48055） */\n  form: 0 | 1 = 0;\n  /** alpha（:6228 初值 255，AI 每子步 -15 地板 0 → 约 9 子步后全显） */\n  private alpha = 255;\n  /** :51209-51214 frameCounter 4 子步一帧，Main.projFrames[601] = 2 */\n  private frame = 0;\n  private frameCounter = 0;\n  /** timeLeft（每 tick 减 1，撞块/出界先死） */\n  private life = BOLT_LIFE;\n  private static frameCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();\n\n  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: 0 | 1) {\n    super();\n    this.x = x - BOLT_W / 2;\n    this.y = y - BOLT_H / 2;\n    this.vx = vx;\n    this.vy = vy;\n    this.form = form;\n  }\n\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (--this.life <= 0) {\n      this.dead = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    const [pr, pg, pb] = portalColor(this.form);\n    const trailColor = this.form === 0 ? '#FFAA28' : '#3CE0FF'; // 与 TrapShot 拖尾同色表（门色近似）\n    let updates = EXTRA_UPDATES;                                  // :15331 numUpdates = extraUpdates\n    while (updates >= 0 && !this.dead) {\n      updates--;                                                  // :15332-15334\n      // ---- AI（:51174-51228，每子步一次）----\n      // :51176-51179 门色×0.5 点光（center + velocity×3）\n      game.addLightAt?.(this.cx + this.vx * 3, this.cy + this.vy * 3,\n        (pr * 0.5) / 255, (pg * 0.5) / 255, (pb * 0.5) / 255);\n      if (this.alpha > 0 && this.alpha <= 15) {                   // :51180-51203：4 尘(264) + 音\n        // 尘速 = velocity×2 + RandomVector2(-1,1) → 定向爆发近似（speed 6 ± 散布）\n        game.spawnBurst?.(this.cx, this.cy, trailColor, 4, 6,\n          Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx), 0.35, { life: 14, damp: 0.94 });\n        // :51195-51202 按 ai[0] 选音（form 1 → Item_114 蓝 / form 0 → Item_115 橙）\n        game.playSfx(this.form === 0 ? 'portalOrange' : 'portalBlue', 1, this.x, this.y);\n      }\n      this.alpha -= 15;                                           // :51204-51208\n      if (this.alpha < 0) this.alpha = 0;\n      if (++this.frameCounter >= 4) {                             // :51209-51214\n        this.frameCounter = 0;\n        this.frame = (this.frame + 1) % 2;\n      }\n      // :51217-51227 alpha==0 期拖尾尘（263，velocity/4，每子步 1 粒）——\n      // 本仓粒子池上限 600（Game.spawnParticles），按 TrapShot 同款惯例折半（每 2 子步 1 粒）\n      if (this.alpha === 0 && (updates & 1) === 0) {\n        game.spawnBurst?.(this.cx, this.cy, trailColor, 1, 0.75,\n          Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx), 0.1, { life: 10, damp: 0.94 });\n      }\n      // ---- 移动 + 撞块（Update 主体序：AI 后 position += velocity，再碰撞响应）----\n      const lvx = this.vx;\n      const lvy = this.vy;\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n      const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);\n      const ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);\n      if (!game.world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) {\n        this.dead = true;                                          // 出界消亡（原版 timeLeft 兜底）\n        break;\n      }\n      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {\n        // :16672-16691 撞块落门\n        if (this.alpha > 0) {                                      // :16674 音门（alpha>0 才播）\n          // :16676-16683 按 ai[1] 选音——601 生成只写 ai0（:63911 尾参），\n          // ai[1] 恒 0 → 此分支恒 Item_115（原版实况，非笔误；AI 侧 :51195 才按 form 分音）\n          game.playSfx('portalOrange', 1, this.x, this.y);\n        }\n        game.placePortal?.(this.x, this.y, lvx, lvy, this.form);   // :16685-16688 TryPlacingPortal(this, lastVelocity, velocity)\n        this.x += this.vx;                                         // :16689 position += velocity\n        this.y += this.vy;\n        this.dead = true;                                          // :16690 Kill()\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_601.png');\n    const [pr, pg, pb] = portalColor(this.form);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    // 与 TrapShot 'portalbolt' 同款朝向约定（速度角，rotOff 0）\n    ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx));\n    ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - this.alpha / 255;                        // alpha 255→0 = 透明→全显\n    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {\n      // GetAlpha（:76199-76202）：601 整体贴图染门色——multiply + destination-in 保形\n      const f = PortalGunBolt.tintedFrame(img, this.frame, pr, pg, pb);\n      ctx.drawImage(f, -10, -10);\n    } else {\n      // 贴图未就绪回退：门色速度向短线（TrapShot 同款兜底形态）\n      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgb(${pr},${pg},${pb})`;\n      ctx.lineWidth = 3;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(-this.vx * 2, -this.vy * 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(0, 0);\n      ctx.stroke();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n  }\n\n  /** 帧染门色缓存（20×19 帧 × 2 帧 × 2 色；手法同 Portal.tintedFrame） */\n  private static tintedFrame(tex: HTMLImageElement, frame: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): HTMLCanvasElement {\n    const key = `${tex.src}|${frame}|${r},${g},${b}`;\n    const hit = PortalGunBolt.frameCache.get(key);\n    if (hit) return hit;\n    const c = document.createElement('canvas');\n    c.width = 20;\n    c.height = 19;\n    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;\n    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 19, 20, 19, 0, 0, 20, 19);\n    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';\n    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n    cx.fillRect(0, 0, 20, 19);\n    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';\n    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 19, 20, 19, 0, 0, 20, 19);\n    PortalGunBolt.frameCache.set(key, c);\n    return c;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 物品 3384 使用入口（Game.updateUse 接线用）：\n *  form = 0 左键（橙门）/ 1 右键（蓝门）——altFunctionUse 语义见 Player.cs:48055。\n *  aimAngle = 屏幕中心→鼠标的瞄准角（Game 侧 atan2(mouseY-canvasH/2, mouseX-canvasW/2) 同款）。\n *  弹速链 1:1：方向 = aimAngle（:63887-63897 按鼠标重瞄准），单子步速 3（:63904 num49=3f），\n *  extraUpdates 30 → PortalGunBolt 内部自跑 31 子步。返回弹体（入 'projectiles' 桶） */\nexport function firePortalGun(\n  game: GameHooks,\n  player: { cx: number; cy: number },\n  aimAngle: number,\n  form: 0 | 1,\n): PortalGunBolt {\n  const mx = player.cx + Math.cos(aimAngle) * 14;   // 出膛位（Game 枪族同款 14px 前置）\n  const my = player.cy - 4 + Math.sin(aimAngle) * 14;\n  let vx = Math.cos(aimAngle) * BOLT_SPEED;          // :63906-63910 normalize(瞄准)×3\n  let vy = Math.sin(aimAngle) * BOLT_SPEED;\n  if (!Number.isFinite(vx) || !Number.isFinite(vy)) {\n    vx = 0;                                          // :63907-63910 NaN → -UnitY\n    vy = -BOLT_SPEED;\n  }\n  const bolt = new PortalGunBolt(mx, my, vx, vy, form);\n  game.spawnParticles?.call?.(0 as never, 0, '#000', 0, 0); // no-op 占位（保持 game 形状探测稳定）\n  return bolt;\n}\n"
}

{
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tests/portal-gun-bolt.test.ts",
 "content": "// 传送门枪物品链 601 PortalGunBolt 对账（src/entities/PortalGunBolt.ts）。\n// 弹体物理锚点：SetDefaults Projectile.cs:6223-6231（10×10/alpha255/extraUpdates30）、\n// 600 发弹 :63883-63911（速度 3）、AI :51174-51228、撞块 :16672-16691。\n// 用假 GameHooks + 手工 TileStore 驱动（免起 dev server，纯逻辑断言）。\nimport { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { PortalGunBolt, firePortalGun } from '../src/entities/PortalGunBolt';\nimport { portalRegistry, clearPortals } from '../src/entities/Portal';\nimport type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';\nimport type { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';\nimport { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';\n\n/** 假 TileStore：只实现 inBounds/isSolid/idx/half/slope（Portal.placePortal 消费面） */\nfunction fakeStore(w: number, h: number, solid: (x: number, y: number) => boolean): TileStore {\n  return {\n    width: w, height: h,\n    inBounds: (x: number, y: number) => x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < w && y < h,\n    isSolid: solid,\n    idx: (x: number, y: number) => y * w + x,\n    half: new Uint8Array(w * h),\n    slope: new Uint8Array(w * h),\n  } as unknown as TileStore;\n}\n\n/** 假 GameHooks：记录 placePortal/sfx/粒子调用 */\nfunction fakeHooks(st: TileStore) {\n  const placed: Array<{ x: number; y: number; vx: number; vy: number; form: number }> = [];\n  const sfx: Array<{ name: string }> = [];\n  const lights: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n  const hooks = {\n    world: { store: st } as GameHooks['world'],\n    placePortal: (x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number) => {\n      placed.push({ x, y, vx, vy, form });\n    },\n    playSfx: (name: string) => { sfx.push({ name }); },\n    addLightAt: (x: number, y: number) => { lights.push([x, y]); },\n  } as unknown as GameHooks;\n  return { hooks, placed, sfx, lights };\n}\n\ndescribe('601 弹体基础（SetDefaults :6223-6231 / 600 发弹 :63883-63911）', () => {\n  it('firePortalGun：出膛位 = 玩家中心 + 14px 前置，弹速 = 方向×3', () => {\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: 100, cy: 200 }, 0, 0);\n    expect(b.x + b.w / 2).toBeCloseTo(114, 5);\n    expect(b.y + b.h / 2).toBeCloseTo(196, 5);\n    expect(b.vx).toBeCloseTo(3, 5);\n    expect(b.vy).toBeCloseTo(0, 5);\n    expect(b.w).toBe(10);\n    expect(b.h).toBe(10);\n    expect(b.form).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('form 1 = 右键蓝门（Player.cs:48055 altFunctionUse==2 → 1）', () => {\n    expect(firePortalGun({ cx: 0, cy: 0 }, -Math.PI / 2, 1).form).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('瞄准 NaN 防御 → -UnitY（:63907-63910）', () => {\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: 0, cy: 0 }, NaN, 0);\n    expect(b.vx).toBe(0);\n    expect(b.vy).toBe(-3);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('601 飞行与撞块（extraUpdates30 → 31 子步/tick；:16672-16691 落门）', () => {\n  it('直线无阻挡：每 tick 位移 = 3×31 = 93px，逐子步不穿墙（alpha 走 -15 台阶）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(400, 400, () => false);\n    const { hooks } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 0);\n    const x0 = b.x;\n    b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(b.x - x0).toBeCloseTo(93, 5);\n    expect(b.dead).toBe(false);\n  });\n  it('撞块 → placePortal(左上, lastVelocity, form) 后 Kill（:16685-16690）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, (x) => x >= 50);  // x≥50 tile 列为墙\n    const { hooks, placed } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 0);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(b.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(placed.length).toBe(1);\n    expect(placed[0].form).toBe(0);\n    expect(placed[0].vx).toBeCloseTo(3, 5);\n    expect(placed[0].vy).toBeCloseTo(0, 5);\n  });\n  it('form 1 撞块同样落门传 form（门色由 placePortal 侧消费）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, (x) => x >= 50);\n    const { hooks, placed } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 1);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(placed[0].form).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('出界消亡（timeLeft 兜底之外的快速路径）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, () => false);\n    const { hooks, placed } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 99, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 0);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(b.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(placed.length).toBe(0);   // 出界不落门\n  });\n  it('端到端：假墙场景 placePortal 挂钩 → 门实体入注册表（Portal.placePortal 解算）', () => {\n    clearPortals();\n    const st = fakeStore(60, 60, (x, y) => y >= 30 && x < 40);  // 下方墙板\n    const reg: unknown[] = [];\n    const { hooks } = fakeHooks(st);\n    // 直接复用真 placePortal（entities/Portal.ts）作挂钩实现，同 Game.placePortal\n    hooks.placePortal = (x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number) => {\n      const p = (require('../src/entities/Portal') as typeof import('../src/entities/Portal'))\n        .placePortal(st, x, y, vx, vy, form);\n      if (p) reg.push(p);\n    };\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, Math.PI / 2, 0);  // 向下射\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(b.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(reg.length).toBe(1);\n    expect(portalRegistry().length).toBe(1);\n    expect(portalRegistry()[0].form).toBe(0);\n    clearPortals();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('601 视觉/音效门（AI :51174-51228 / 撞块 :16672-16684）', () => {\n  it('撞块音走 ai[1] 恒 0 分支 → 恒 Item_115（portalOrange）', () => {\n    // 近距墙面：飞行 <16 子步即撞（alpha 仍 >0）→ 走 :16674 音门\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, (x) => x >= 13);\n    const { hooks, sfx } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 12, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 0);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 10 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(b.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(sfx.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n    expect(sfx.every((s) => s.name === 'portalOrange')).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('远距飞行：alpha 跨 15 阈值时按 form 分音（form1 → portalBlue, :51195-51202）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, (x) => x >= 90);   // 远墙\n    const { hooks, sfx } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 1);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 120 && !b.dead; t++) b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(sfx.some((s) => s.name === 'portalBlue')).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('点光按门色×0.5 逐子步登记（:51176-51179）', () => {\n    const st = fakeStore(100, 100, () => false);\n    const { hooks, lights } = fakeHooks(st);\n    const b = firePortalGun({ cx: TILE * 10, cy: TILE * 10 }, 0, 0);\n    b.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(lights.length).toBe(31);   // 31 子步每步一光\n  });\n});\n"
}

82	      case 1: give(586, rnd.next(20, 50)); break;             // Candy Cane Block
83	      default: give(591, rnd.next(20, 50)); break;            // Green Candy Cane Block
84	    }
85	  }
86	}
87	
88	/** OpenLegacyPresent（Player.cs:6772-6788）：旧版三色礼盒 599/600/601——单掷 14 支 */
89	function openLegacyPresent(give: BagGive, hardMode: boolean, rnd: BagRand): void {
90	  const num = rnd.next(14);                                   // :6775
91	  if (num === 0 && hardMode) {                                // :6776-6779 0/14 Snow Globe（硬模式门）
92	    give(602);
93	  } else if (num <= 7) {                                      // :6780-6783 1-7 → Candy Cane Block
94	    give(586, rnd.next(20, 50));
95	  } else {                                                    // :6784-6787 8-13 → Green Candy Cane Block
96	    give(591, rnd.next(20, 50));
97	  }
98	}
99	
100	/** OpenGoodieBag（Player.cs:9199-9282）：礼袋 1774。前四支独立掷后 early return
101	 *  （:9205/9210/9215/9220），兜底 switch(Next(19)) 十九套装（QuickSpawnItem_CostumeSet
102	 *  :9175-9186 = 逐件 QuickSpawnItem） */
103	function openGoodieBag(give: BagGive, rnd: BagRand): void {
104	  if (rnd.next(150) === 0) { give(1810); return; }            // :9202-9205 Unlucky Yarn 1810
105	  if (rnd.next(150) === 0) { give(1800); return; }            // :9207-9210 Bat Hook 1800
106	  if (rnd.next(4) === 0) { give(1809, rnd.next(10, 41)); return; } // :9212-9215 Rotten Egg 1809×10-40
107	  if (rnd.next(10) === 0) { give(rnd.next(1846, 1851)); return; }  // :9217-9220 挂画 1846-1850（Next(1846,1851) 闭开）
108	  switch (rnd.next(19)) {                                     // :9222-9281 化装套装
109	    case 0: give(1749); give(1750); give(1751); break;        // Cat 三件
110	    case 1: give(1746); give(1747); give(1748); break;        // Creeper 三件
111	    case 2: give(1752); give(1753); break;                    // Ghost 头/衣
112	    case 3: give(1767); give(1768); give(1769); break;        // Leprechaun 三件
113	    case 4: give(1770); give(1771); break;                    // Pixie 衣/裤
114	    case 5: give(1772); give(1773); break;                    // Princess 帽/裙
115	    case 6: give(1754); give(1755); give(1756); break;        // Pumpkin 三件
116	    case 7: give(1757); give(1758); give(1759); break;        // Robot 三件
117	    case 8: give(1760); give(1761); give(1762); break;        // Unicorn 三件
118	    case 9: give(1763); give(1764); give(1765); break;        // Vampire 三件
119	    case 10: give(1766); give(1775); give(1776); break;       // Witch 帽+裙+靴
120	    case 11: give(1777); give(1778); break;                   // Bride of Frankenstein 头/裙
121	    case 12: give(1779); give(1780); give(1781); break;       // Karate Tortoise 三件
122	    case 13: give(1819); give(1820); break;                   // Reaper 兜帽/长袍
123	    case 14: give(1821); give(1822); give(1823); break;       // Fox 三件
124	    case 15: give(1824); break;                               // Cat Ears
125	    case 16: give(1838); give(1839); give(1840); break;       // Space Creature 三件
126	    case 17: give(1841); give(1842); give(1843); break;       // Wolf 三件
127	    default: give(1851); give(1852); break;                   // Treasure Hunter 衣/裤
128	  }
129	}
130	
131	/** OpenHerbBag（Player.cs:9064-9138）：草药袋 3093——2-4 件（1/3 +1），每件
132	 *  Next(14) 七草七籽映射 + 2-4 栈（1/3 再 +1-4） */
133	function openHerbBag(give: BagGive, rnd: BagRand): void {
134	  let num = rnd.next(2, 5);                                   // :9067 件数 2-4
135	  if (rnd.next(3) === 0) num++;                               // :9068-9071 1/3 → +1
136	  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {                             // :9072
137	    let id = rnd.next(14);                                    // :9074
138	    switch (id) {                                             // :9075-9130 十四支映射（七草七籽）
139	      case 0: id = 313; break;   // Daybloom
140	      case 1: id = 314; break;   // Moonglow
141	      case 2: id = 315; break;   // Blinkroot
142	      case 3: id = 317; break;   // Waterleaf（注意 317≠316 顺序对调）
143	      case 4: id = 316; break;   // Deathweed
144	      case 5: id = 318; break;   // Fireblossom
145	      case 6: id = 2358; break;  // Shiverthorn
146	      case 7: id = 307; break;   // Daybloom Seeds
147	      case 8: id = 308; break;   // Moonglow Seeds
148	      case 9: id = 309; break;   // Blinkroot Seeds
149	      case 10: id = 311; break;  // Waterleaf Seeds
150	      case 11: id = 310; break;  // Deathweed Seeds
151	      case 12: id = 312; break;  // Fireblossom Seeds
152	      default: id = 2357; break; // Shiverthorn Seeds
153	    }
154	    let stack = rnd.next(2, 5);                               // :9131 栈 2-4
155	    if (rnd.next(3) === 0) stack += rnd.next(1, 5);           // :9132-9134 1/3 → +1-4
156	    give(id, stack);
157	  }
158	}
159	
160	/** 右键开包分派（TryOpenContainer_GrantItems :1662-1721 同源子集）：
161	 *  返回 true = 已开（调用方做 stack-- + PlaySound(7)）；false = 非本引擎物品
162	 *  （宝藏袋/木匣/钓鱼箱等走各自链，UI 应回落原右键行为） */
163	export function openBagItem(
164	  vid: number,
165	  give: BagGive,
166	  world: { hardMode: boolean },
167	  rng: BagRand = defaultRand,
168	): boolean {
169	  switch (vid) {
170	    case 1869: openPresent(give, world.hardMode, rng); return true;
171	    case 599: case 600: case 601: openLegacyPresent(give, world.hardMode, rng); return true;
172	    case 1774: openGoodieBag(give, rng); return true;
173	    case 3093: openHerbBag(give, rng); return true;
174	    default: return false;
175	  }
176	}
177	
178	/** give 出口的 id 是否在本仓已注册（防 vanilla.json 缺条目静默吞掉） */
179	export function bagItemIdRegistered(id: number): boolean {
180	  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id] !== undefined;
181	}
182	

```

146	  private static tintedFrame(tex: HTMLImageElement, frame: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
147	    const key = `${tex.src}|${frame}|${r},${g},${b}`;
148	    const hit = PortalGunBolt.frameCache.get(key);
149	    if (hit) return hit;
150	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
151	    c.width = 20;

153	    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
154	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 19, 20, 19, 0, 0, 20, 19);
155	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
156	    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
157	    cx.fillRect(0, 0, 20, 19);
158	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
159	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, frame * 19, 20, 19, 0, 0, 20, 19);
160	    PortalGunBolt.frameCache.set(key, c);
161	    return c;
162	  }
163	}

165	/** 物品 3384 使用入口（Game.updateUse 接线用）：
166	 *  form = 0 左键（橙门）/ 1 右键（蓝门）——altFunctionUse 语义见 Player.cs:48055。
167	 *  aimAngle = 屏幕中心→鼠标的瞄准角（Game 侧 atan2(mouseY-canvasH/2, mouseX-canvasW/2) 同款）。
168	 *  弹速链 1:1：方向 = aimAngle（:63887-63897 按鼠标重瞄准），单子步速 3（:63904 num49=3f），
169	 *  extraUpdates 30 → PortalGunBolt 内部自跑 31 子步。返回弹体（调用方入 'projectiles' 桶） */
170	export function firePortalGun(
171	  player: { cx: number; cy: number },
172	  aimAngle: number,
173	  form: 0 | 1,
174	): PortalGunBolt {
175	  const mx = player.cx + Math.cos(aimAngle) * 14;   // 出膛位（Game 枪族同款 14px 前置）
176	  const my = player.cy - 4 + Math.sin(aimAngle) * 14;
177	  let vx = Math.cos(aimAngle) * BOLT_SPEED;          // :63906-63910 normalize(瞄准)×3
178	  let vy = Math.sin(aimAngle) * BOLT_SPEED;
179	  if (!Number.isFinite(vx) || !Number.isFinite(vy)) {
180	    vx = 0;                                          // :63907-63910 NaN → -UnitY
181	    vy = -BOLT_SPEED;
182	  }
183	  const bolt = new PortalGunBolt(mx, my, vx, vy, form);
184	  return bolt;

```
import：`import { firePortalGun } from '../entities/PortalGunBolt';`。**不要加发射音**（UseSound=null）。

**B2. 右键发射（蓝门 form 1）** — `updateUse()` 内 `if (inp.rightDown) { ... }` 块**末尾**（宝箱/NPC/门等地块交互分派之后，= 原版 :31070 `!tileInteractionHappened` 语义——右键悬门/箱时优先交互）：

```ts
if (inp.rightDown && heldDef && viIdFromKey(heldDef.key) === 3384 && this.player.useTime === 0) {
  const ang = Math.atan2(inp.mouseY - this.renderer.canvas.height / 2,
                         inp.mouseX - this.renderer.canvas.width / 2);
  this.player.facing = Math.cos(ang) > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  this.entities.add(firePortalGun(this.player, ang, 1), 'projectiles');  // Player.cs:48055 altFunctionUse==2 → 1
  this.useSwing(heldDef, ang);
  this.player.useTime = 20;
  this.input.rightDown = false;
}
```
接线前物品静默无动作（数据侧 null 门已防废弹），不会误发。

3. PortalGunBolt 未做联机同步（TrapShot 有 msg27 bit7）——门实体本就未同步，沿用现状

5. 宝藏袋右键开包未接（本引擎 `openBagItem` 对 3328 等返回 false 回落原行为；宝藏袋仍走现有手持左键链 Game.ts:5759）
